([hazlijohar95/claude-tax-malaysia](https://github.com/hazlijohar95/claude-tax-malaysia/tree/HEAD/indirect-tax/skills/einvoice) · ★1)
- **einvoice-export**: Exports EU-compliant structured electronic invoices from Exact Online or manual input. Supports ZUGFeRD/XRechnung (Germany), UBL 2.1 / PEPPOL BIS (Netherlands, Belgium, and pan-EU). Validates against the EN16931 European e-invoicing standard, flags validation errors in plain English, and produces a downloadable XML file ready for submission to the recipient or a government portal. Mandatory in Germany for B2B since 2025; mandatory across the EU for large companies, rolling out to SMBs by 2028. Trigger when the user says: "e-invoice," "electronic invoice," "ZUGFeRD," "XRechnung," "UBL invoice,… ([artdaw/small-business-eu](https://github.com/artdaw/small-business-eu/tree/HEAD/skills/einvoice-export))
- **einvoice-from-export**: Read an ERP invoice export (CSV, Excel or UBL XML), map its columns to the UAE or Saudi (ZATCA) e-invoice check columns, and run the check. Use inside einvoice-readiness or einvoice-ksa when invoices come from an ERP such as Zoho Books, Tally Prime, SAP Business One, Odoo, Dynamics 365 Business Central, QuickBooks or Xero. ([Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check](https://github.com/Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check/tree/HEAD/skills/einvoice-from-export))
- **einvoice-from-salesforce**: Pull a read-only sample of invoices, lines and customers from Salesforce (Salesforce Billing, Revenue Cloud or custom invoice objects) and turn it into the CSV the UAE or Saudi (ZATCA) e-invoice check reads. Use inside einvoice-readiness or einvoice-ksa when invoices live in Salesforce. ([Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check](https://github.com/Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check/tree/HEAD/skills/einvoice-from-salesforce))
- **einvoice-integration**: Use when a developer is adding e-Invoice (IRN) generation, cancellation, lookup or QR code to their code through WhiteBooks, or asks to generate or cancel an IRN directly. Covers the NIC sign-in, duplicate IRN handling (error 2150), the not-found read-back (2154), and what to do when a generate call times out. Also use for any e-Invoice question, with or without a project, such as what a field or error code means, which endpoint to call, the JSON body for an invoice, or a sample request in curl or any language. ([chandraxdev/whitebooksdev](https://github.com/chandraxdev/whitebooksdev/tree/HEAD/skills/einvoice-integration))
- **einvoice-ksa**: Pre-check Saudi Arabia (ZATCA, Fatoora) invoice data before it becomes e-invoice XML, and list the gaps per invoice with the official rule id (BR-KSA-..., BR-...), a fix and an owner. Use when someone asks if their Saudi invoice data is ready for ZATCA e-invoicing, or to check invoices for VAT number, transaction code, address, VAT category or credit-note problems. Never signs, sends, reports or clears invoices. ([Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check](https://github.com/Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check/tree/HEAD/skills/einvoice-ksa))
- **einvoice-master-data**: Audit a UAE company's customer list (TRN, TIN, country, emirate) and item list (goods or services, HS or SAC code, unit code, VAT category) for UAE e-invoicing (PINT AE) before invoices are issued. Use when someone wants to clean customer or product master data in Salesforce or an ERP for e-invoicing. Read-only; never changes records. ([Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check](https://github.com/Mindcat-app/uae-einvoice-check/tree/HEAD/skills/einvoice-master-data))
- **einvoice-matching**: Cómo se procesa la factura electrónica de cada país del registro —la DIAN, el SRI, la SUNAT, el SII, el SAT, la NF-e, ARCA, la DGI de Panamá, la FEL, el comprobante de Hacienda, la factura de Honduras con su CAI y el documento electrónico de SIFEN— y cómo se cruza con la contabilidad: los campos que se leen y su fuente, las reglas del cruce, los duplicados y lo que queda sin opinión fiscal. Úsalo al revisar las facturas del periodo, al explicar por qué una factura no cruza o cuando un documento no se pudo procesar. E-invoicing, DIAN, SRI, SUNAT, CUFE, clave de acceso, CAI, CDC, timbrado. ([josenanez/criterio](https://github.com/josenanez/criterio/tree/HEAD/plugins/criterio-finance/skills/einvoice-matching) · ★1)

Produces a populated matrix, not a framework description. Use when the user asks about prioritizing tasks by urgency and importance, needs help deciding what to work on first, or wants to sort a task list using the Eisenhower method. Do NOT use for weighted scoring prioritization (use task-prioritization), time-blocking scheduled tasks (use time-blocking), or enterprise resource allocation (use business strategy skills). - **eisenhower-matrix**: "What is important is seldom urgent and what is urgent is seldom important." Master Dwight D. Eisenhower's prioritization framework to focus on what truly matters.
